Burst LOS setup, 
HectonDirectorAI.cs (line 341)
:

[BurstCompile(FloatMode = FloatMode.Fast, FloatPrecision = FloatPrecision.Standard)]
internal struct PredatorSightRaycastBuildJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<PredatorSightRaycastInput> Inputs;
    public NativeArray<RaycastCommand> Commands;
    public int RequestCount;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        if (index >= RequestCount)
        {
            Commands[index] = new RaycastCommand(
                Vector3.zero,
                Vector3.forward,
                new QueryParameters(HectonLayerMasks.NoLayers, false, QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore, false),
                0f);
            return;
        }

        PredatorSightRaycastInput input = Inputs[index];
        float3 delta = input.Target - input.Origin;
        float length = math.max(0.001f, math.length(delta));
        float3 direction = delta / length;
        Commands[index] = new RaycastCommand(
            new Vector3(input.Origin.x, input.Origin.y, input.Origin.z),
            new Vector3(direction.x, direction.y, direction.z),
            new QueryParameters(input.LayerMask, false, QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore, false),
            math.min(input.MaxDistance, length));
    }
}
Schedule site: 
HectonDirectorAI.cs (line 825)
.

[BurstCompile(FloatMode = FloatMode.Fast, FloatPrecision = FloatPrecision.Standard)]
internal struct PredatorSightRaycastBuildJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<PredatorSightRaycastInput> Inputs;
    public NativeArray<RaycastCommand> Commands;
    public int RequestCount;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        if (index >= RequestCount)
        {
            Commands[index] = new RaycastCommand(
                Vector3.zero,
                Vector3.forward,
                new QueryParameters(HectonLayerMasks.NoLayers, false, QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore, false),
                0f);
            return;
        }

        PredatorSightRaycastInput input = Inputs[index];
        float3 delta = input.Target - input.Origin;
        float length = math.max(0.001f, math.length(delta));
        float3 direction = delta / length;
        Commands[index] = new RaycastCommand(
            new Vector3(input.Origin.x, input.Origin.y, input.Origin.z),
            new Vector3(direction.x, direction.y, direction.z),
            new QueryParameters(input.LayerMask, false, QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore, false),
            math.min(input.MaxDistance, length));
    }
}
Scheduled at 
HectonDirectorAI.cs (line 837)
 with RaycastCommand.ScheduleBatch.

+        private static bool IsPredatorBehindPlayerViewByAup(
+            in AbsoluteUniversePosition playerAup,
+            in AbsoluteUniversePosition predatorAup,
+            Vector3 playerForward)
+        {
+            double3 predatorDelta = predatorAup.ToAbsoluteDouble3() - playerAup.ToAbsoluteDouble3();
+            float3 runtimeDelta = new float3((float)predatorDelta.x, (float)predatorDelta.y, (float)predatorDelta.z);
+            float distanceSqr = math.lengthsq(runtimeDelta);
+            if (distanceSqr <= 0.0001f)
+                return false;
+
+            float3 directionToPredator = runtimeDelta * math.rsqrt(distanceSqr);
+            float3 safeForward = math.normalizesafe((float3)playerForward, new float3(0f, 0f, 1f));
+            return math.dot(safeForward, directionToPredator) <= PredatorSightRearViewDotThreshold;
+        }

What Changed

Added real triplanar base/silt sampling with hard blend weights in 
Hecton_AbyssalVoxelRock.shader (line 318)
:
half3 ResolveTriplanarBlendWeights(half3 normalWS)
{
    half3 blend = pow(max(abs(normalWS), half3(0.0001h, 0.0001h, 0.0001h)), max((half)_TriplanarBlendSharpness, 8.0h));
    return blend / max(dot(blend, half3(1.0h, 1.0h, 1.0h)), 0.0001h);
}

half4 SampleTriplanarColor(TEXTURE2D_PARAM(tex, samp), float3 positionWS, half3 normalWS)
{
    float tiling = max(_Tiling, 0.0001);
    half3 blend = ResolveTriplanarBlendWeights(normalWS);
    half4 xSample = SAMPLE_TEXTURE2D(tex, samp, positionWS.zy * tiling);
    half4 ySample = SAMPLE_TEXTURE2D(tex, samp, positionWS.xz * tiling);
    half4 zSample = SAMPLE_TEXTURE2D(tex, samp, positionWS.xy * tiling);
    return (xSample * blend.x) + (ySample * blend.y) + (zSample * blend.z);
}
Kept world-space GPU jagged displacement via existing ValueNoise3 path and cave-mouth displacement.
Added cheap cave-mouth AO multiply from distance-derived COLOR.a / skirt alpha at shader lines 820-822.
Silt dust now uses triplanar silt texture on upward normals at line 824.
Set laser debris lifetime to 5s in 
VoxelDeltaProcessor.cs (line 66)
.
Debris now uses authored sink distance/duration, disables simulated collision, and settles as instanced static state in 
DebrisManager.cs (line 432)
.

Surgery Log

float ResolveOrganicVertexDisplacement(float3 absolutePositionWS, float3 normalOS, half seamMask)
{
    float scale = max(_OrganicDisplacementScale, 0.02);
    float lowNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * scale + 3.17);
    float midNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * (scale * 2.03) + 19.71);
    float fineNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * max(_OrganicDisplacementFineScale, 0.05) + 53.2);
    float ridged = abs(((lowNoise * 0.54 + midNoise * 0.31 + fineNoise * 0.15) * 2.0) - 1.0);
    float signedRidge = (ridged * 2.0) - 1.0;
    float slopeGate = saturate(1.0 - abs(normalOS.y) * 0.28);
    float seamBoost = 1.0 + saturate(seamMask) * _OrganicDisplacementSeamBoost;
    return signedRidge * _OrganicDisplacementStrength * seamBoost * slopeGate;
}

{

}

float2 HectonResolveTerrainBumpOffset(half3 viewDirectionWS, half dominantAxis, half height01)
{
    half strength = saturate((half)_BumpOffsetStrength);
    if (strength <= 0.0001h)
        return float2(0.0, 0.0);

    half3 viewDir = normalize(viewDirectionWS);
    float2 projectedView;
    half axisDepth;
    if (dominantAxis < 0.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.zy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.x); }
    else if (dominantAxis > 1.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.xy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.z); }
    else { projectedView = viewDir.xz; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.y); }

    half centeredHeight = height01 - saturate((half)_BumpOffsetHeightCenter);
    return -projectedView * (centeredHeight * strength / max(axisDepth, 0.25h));
}

float2 HectonResolveTerrainBumpOffset(half3 viewDirectionWS, half dominantAxis, half height01)
{
    half strength = saturate((half)_BumpOffsetStrength);
    if (strength <= 0.0001h)
        return float2(0.0, 0.0);

    half3 viewDir = normalize(viewDirectionWS);
    float2 projectedView;
    half axisDepth;

    if (dominantAxis < 0.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.zy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.x); }
    else if (dominantAxis > 1.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.xy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.z); }
    else { projectedView = viewDir.xz; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.y); }

    half centeredHeight = height01 - saturate((half)_BumpOffsetHeightCenter);
    return -projectedView * (centeredHeight * strength / max(axisDepth, 0.25h));
}

Bump Offset HLSL

float2 HectonResolveTerrainBumpOffset(half3 viewDirectionWS, half dominantAxis, half height01)
{
    half strength = saturate((half)_BumpOffsetStrength);
    if (strength <= 0.0001h)
        return float2(0.0, 0.0);

    half3 viewDir = normalize(viewDirectionWS);
    float2 projectedView;
    half axisDepth;
    if (dominantAxis < 0.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.zy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.x); }
    else if (dominantAxis > 1.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.xy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.z); }
    else { projectedView = viewDir.xz; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.y); }

    half centeredHeight = height01 - saturate((half)_BumpOffsetHeightCenter);
    return -projectedView * (centeredHeight * strength / max(axisDepth, 0.25h));
}

Bump Offset HLSL

float2 HectonResolveTerrainBumpOffset(half3 viewDirectionWS, half dominantAxis, half height01)
{
    half strength = saturate((half)_BumpOffsetStrength);
    if (strength <= 0.0001h)
        return float2(0.0, 0.0);

    half3 viewDir = normalize(viewDirectionWS);
    float2 projectedView;
    half axisDepth;

    if (dominantAxis < 0.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.zy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.x); }
    else if (dominantAxis > 1.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.xy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.z); }
    else { projectedView = viewDir.xz; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.y); }

    half centeredHeight = height01 - saturate((half)_BumpOffsetHeightCenter);
    return -projectedView * (centeredHeight * strength / max(axisDepth, 0.25h));
}
AAA Cheat
Physical surface detail got demoted to math: terrain normal texture-array sampling was removed, leaving one albedo/height array plus one-step bump offset. Scooter shaft pixels with weak contribution bypass the 8-tap blur and use one sampled fake tap.

AUP rebase Burst job:

[BurstCompile(FloatMode = FloatMode.Fast, FloatPrecision = FloatPrecision.Standard)]
private struct WreckMatrixRebaseJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    public NativeArray<Matrix4x4> Matrices;
    public float3 RuntimeOffset;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        Matrix4x4 matrix = Matrices[index];
        matrix.m03 += RuntimeOffset.x;
        matrix.m13 += RuntimeOffset.y;
        matrix.m23 += RuntimeOffset.z;
        Matrices[index] = matrix;
    }
}

MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: SAVE SYSTEM & PERSISTENCE
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: THE CRUCIBLE V3 / TRANSPARENT PERSISTENCE
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (AUP, Zero-GC, DOD, MX350 Cinematic Cheats)[0. SITREP: REALITY CHECK]
The Fort Knox vault is open. MMF infinite append is active. Now make the UX invisible. The player should never wait for a save.

[I. MANDATORY RECONNAISSANCE]
1. Scan SaveBinaryStorage.cs, SaveManager.cs, and SaveThumbnailSystem.cs.
2. Verify UnsafeUtility.MemCpy is used for MMF writes. NO managed byte[] staging.
3. Check PlayerInventory.cs for the dirty-bit logic.

[II. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: INVISIBLE ARCHIVE]
1. THUMBNAIL CAPTURE OPTIMIZATION: AsyncGPUReadback is ONLY requested if camera moved >5m or rotated >5° since last save. Duplicate requests rejected.
2. DIEGETIC HUD NOTIFICATIONS: Remove "Saving..." text. Use a blinking "DATA" suit-icon in SuitHUDV4CanvasOverlay driven by MappedWriteStarted events.
3. BACKGROUND COMPRESSION THROTTLE: If save compression job > 10ms, pause and resume on next SlowTick. CPU priority belongs to the player.
4. INVENTORY DIRTY-BIT FLUSH: Inventory DTO writes to MMF ONLY if _isDirty is true. Flag clears only after successful mapped write commit.
5. FAIL-SAFE SECTOR RESET: If indexed sector is corrupt, instantly re-run WorldGen for that sector and overwrite bad data. Display: "CRITICAL ERROR: LOCALIZED DATA CORRUPTION. TERRAIN RE-INITIALIZED."
6. VERSIONED SEED GLITCH: If WorldSeed mismatches, trigger "UNSTABLE REALITY" HUD glitch and chromatic aberration pulse.
7. DIFFERENTIAL INVENTORY SAVE: Do not diff slots. Overwrite the entire 48-byte Inventory block in MMF when dirty.
8. TOMBSTONE PROTOCOL: Deleted Entity IDs are saved as tombstones. Load path registers deleted UIDs before active records are hydrated.

Time-Sliced Hydration Loop

private async Awaitable RunHydrationSessionAsync(int sessionVersion)
{
    while (Application.isPlaying &&
           ReferenceEquals(GlobalRegistry.PersistentWorldRegistry, this) &&
           _hydrationSessionVersion == sessionVersion)
    {
        if (!TryProcessHydrationBurst())
            break;

        await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(cancellationToken: destroyCancellationToken);
    }
}

private bool TryProcessHydrationBurst()
{
    long budgetDeadline = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() + HydrationFrameBudgetTicks;
    while (processedCount < MaxHydrationsPerFrame && _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length)
    {
        if (processedCount > 0 && Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() >= budgetDeadline)
            break;

        // apply one hydration record
    }

    return _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length;
}

What Was Wrong

The hydration path still had one GC landmine: PersistentWorldRegistry.TryResolveItemData() could fall back through record.ItemPersistentId.ToString() after a hash miss. That path is reachable during hydration apply, so it was not acceptable.

Unity also had stale compile blockers after domain reload: missing celestial registry compatibility and a stale Bee DAG reference. I fixed the real source gap and forced Unity to rebuild script inputs.

What I Did

Replaced managed persistent-id string fallback with fixed-buffer FNV hashing over FixedString128Bytes.
Added hydration performance telemetry: GlobalTelemetryBus.PublishPerformanceWarning when hydration apply exceeds 0.2ms.
Added registry-backed HectonCelestialEngine.ActiveRuntimeInstance, no local singleton ownership.
Reset GlobalRegistry celestial slot on subsystem registration.
Updated persistence smoke checks for the GC purge and telemetry hook.
Time-sliced hydration loop:

private async Awaitable RunHydrationSessionAsync(int sessionVersion)
{
    while (Application.isPlaying &&
           ReferenceEquals(GlobalRegistry.PersistentWorldRegistry, this) &&
           _hydrationSessionVersion == sessionVersion)
    {
        if (!TryProcessHydrationBurst())
            break;

        await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(cancellationToken: destroyCancellationToken);
    }
}

private bool TryProcessHydrationBurst()
{
    long burstStartTicks = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
    long budgetDeadline = burstStartTicks + HydrationFrameBudgetTicks; // Stopwatch.Frequency / 250
    int processedCount = 0;

    while (processedCount < MaxHydrationsPerFrame &&
           _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length)
    {
        if (processedCount > 0 && Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() >= budgetDeadline)
            break;

        ApplyOneHydrationRecord(_pendingHydrationRecords[_pendingHydrationReadIndex++]);
        processedCount++;
    }

    if (processedCount > 0)
        PublishHydrationBudgetWarningIfNeeded(Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() - burstStartTicks);

    return _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length;
}
AAA Cheat

Deleted the honest managed-string identity fallback. Replacement: hash the fixed persistent-id bytes directly and use hash lookup only. It is cheaper, deterministic, and avoids hydration-path garbage.

long burstStartTicks = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();

int processedCount = 0;
while (processedCount < MaxHydrationsPerFrame &&

    if (!IsValidRecordIndex(recordIndex))

    PersistentWorldItemRecord record = _records[recordIndex];

    HydrateRecord(recordIndex, in record);

    long budgetDeadline = burstStartTicks + HydrationFrameBudgetTicks;

    while (processedCount < MaxHydrationsPerFrame &&
           _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length)
    {
        // apply one hydration record

        if (Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() >= budgetDeadline)
        {
            PublishHydrationBudgetWarningIfNeeded(Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() - burstStartTicks);
            return true;
        }
    }

    return _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length;
}

private void UpdateSunPosition(float dt)
{
    if (sunLight == null) { _sunDirectionResolvedFromMatrix = false; return; }

    UpdateInternalOrbit(dt);

    float3 axis = math.normalizesafe((float3)sunOrbitAxis, new float3(1, 0, 0));
    _sunOrbitRotationMatrix = BuildAxisAngleRotationMatrix(axis, math.radians(_accumulatedOrbitalAngle));
    _resolvedSunForward = _sunOrbitRotationMatrix.MultiplyVector(Vector3.forward);

    sunLight.transform.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(_resolvedSunForward);
    _resolvedSunDirection = new float3(-_resolvedSunForward.x, -_resolvedSunForward.y, -_resolvedSunForward.z);
    _sunDirectionResolvedFromMatrix = true;
}

float2 ComputeScrollingRainNormal(float2 uv, float2 windDir, float windSpeed, float rainIntensity)
{
    float2 normalUV = uv * float2(28.0, 58.0);
    normalUV.x += uv.y * (1.4 + windDir.x * 2.6) + windDir.x * _Time.y * 1.7;
    normalUV.y -= _Time.y * (7.5 + windSpeed * 7.0);

    float height = ValueNoise(normalUV);
    float heightX = ValueNoise(normalUV + float2(0.071, 0.0)) - height;
    float heightY = ValueNoise(normalUV + float2(0.0, 0.071)) - height;

    return float2(heightX + windDir.x * 0.05, heightY - 0.08) * (rainIntensity * 0.0065);
}

float2 ComputeScrollingRainNormal(float2 uv, float2 windDir, float windSpeed, float rainIntensity)
{
    float2 normalUV = uv * float2(28.0, 58.0);
    normalUV.x += uv.y * (1.4 + windDir.x * 2.6) + windDir.x * _Time.y * 1.7;
    normalUV.y -= _Time.y * (7.5 + windSpeed * 7.0);

    float heightX = ValueNoise(normalUV + float2(0.071, 0.0)) - height;
    float heightY = ValueNoise(normalUV + float2(0.0, 0.071)) - height;

    return float2(heightX + windDir.x * 0.05, heightY - 0.08) * (rainIntensity * 0.0065);
}

BootstrapRegistryCycleValidator.cs (line 1)

Core implementation added:

while (remainingNodeCount > 0)
{
    int selectedNodeIndex = -1;
    for (int nodeIndex = 0; nodeIndex < remainingNodeCount; nodeIndex++)
    {
        GlobalRegistryServiceSlot candidate = remainingNodes[nodeIndex];
        if (incomingCounts[(int)candidate] == 0)
        {
            selectedNodeIndex = nodeIndex;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (selectedNodeIndex < 0)
    {
        ReportCycle(remainingNodes, remainingNodeCount, incomingCounts);
        return false;
    }

    GlobalRegistryServiceSlot selected = remainingNodes[selectedNodeIndex];
    executionOrder[executionOrderCount++] = selected;

    remainingNodeCount--;
    remainingNodes[selectedNodeIndex] = remainingNodes[remainingNodeCount];

    for (int edgeIndex = 0; edgeIndex < _startupEdges.Length; edgeIndex++)
    {
        if (_startupEdges[edgeIndex].Dependency == selected)
            incomingCounts[(int)_startupEdges[edgeIndex].Service]--;
    }
}

Kahn Validator
Complete source: 
BootstrapRegistryCycleValidator.cs (line 35)

Core validator path:

public static bool TryBuildExecutionOrder(
    GlobalRegistryServiceSlot[] nodes,
    BootstrapRegistryDependencyEdge[] edges,
    GlobalRegistryServiceSlot[] executionOrder,
    out int executionOrderCount)
{
    executionOrderCount = 0;
    if (nodes == null || edges == null || executionOrder == null)
        return false;

    int nodeCount = nodes.Length;
    if (executionOrder.Length < nodeCount || nodeCount > _inDegreeScratch.Length)
        return false;

    lock (_validationScratchLock)
    {
        return TryBuildExecutionOrderLocked(nodes, edges, executionOrder, nodeCount, out executionOrderCount);
    }
}

7-Step Raymarch HLSL

int stepCount = clamp((int)round(_HectonVolumetricMarchParams.y), 1, 7);
float stepSize = rayLength * SafeRcp((float)stepCount);
float jitter = ResolveBlueNoise(screenUv) * stepSize * saturate(_HectonVolumetricMarchParams.z);

[loop]
for (int stepIndex = 0; stepIndex < 7; stepIndex++)
{
    if (stepIndex >= stepCount)
        break;

    float travelDistance = min(rayLength, jitter + (stepIndex + 0.5) * stepSize);
    float3 samplePositionWS = scenePositionWS + rayDirectionWS * travelDistance;
    float density = SampleFogDensity(samplePositionWS, saturate(travelDistance / max(rayLength, 0.0001)));
    if (density <= 0.0001)
        continue;

    float coneDither = lerp(0.58, 1.22, ResolveBlueNoise(screenUv + float2(stepIndex * 0.071, stepIndex * 0.113)));
    accumulatedLight += density * localScatterCoeff * flashlightCone * coneDither * transmittance * stepSize * flashlightColor;
}

Surgery Log

// Splat Burst logic
slopeDegrees = degrees(atan(sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz)));
rock = smoothstep(RockSlopeThreshold - blend, RockSlopeThreshold + blend, slopeDegrees);
cavity = saturate((neighborAverage - center) * heightScale * CavityStrength);
sediment = saturate(sedimentMask * SedimentStrength);
silt = saturate(max(cavity, sediment) * (1 - rock));
sand = saturate((1 - rock) * (1 - silt));
weights = normalize(float3(sand, rock, silt));
// 50m biome border smoothstep
t = saturate(1f - abs(distanceFromBorderMeters) / 50f);
blend01 = 0.5f * t * t * (3f - 2f * t);
blend255 = round(blend01 * 255f); // border center => 128

FNV-1a Kernel
Current Burst-addressable quest hash kernel is in 
QuestRuntimeTypes.cs (line 166)
:

[BurstCompile]
internal static unsafe class QuestFlagHashKernel
{
    public static uint ComputeStableHash(ReadOnlySpan<char> value)
    {
        if (value.Length <= 0)
            return 0u;

        fixed (char* valuePtr = value)
            return ComputeStableHash((ushort*)valuePtr, value.Length);
    }

    [BurstCompile]
    public static uint ComputeStableHash(ushort* value, int length)
    {
        if (value == null || length <= 0)
            return 0u;

        unchecked
        {
            uint hash = Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvOffsetBasis;
            for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
            {
                ushort current = value[i];
                hash ^= (byte)current;
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
                hash ^= (byte)(current >> 8);
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
            }

            return hash;
        }
    }
}

Surgery Math

Look hot-swap уже был корректный: t = saturate(elapsed / 0.25), ease t*t*(3-2*t), direction через math.slerp(fromRotation, toRotation, eased), magnitude separately blended/fallback lerp.
Blue-noise dissolve: coverage = 1 - smoothstep(elapsed / 2.5), shader samples _BlueNoiseTex in screen-space with temporal R2 offset, then alpha = step(noise, coverage).
Earthquake heave: snoise(noiseTime * 0.28, seed + 17.13) * axisY * scale * 0.35, added after normal shake accumulation and clamped before camera local offset.

Что изменено/проверено

Event arteries уже в текущем дереве: critical-порядок перед ambient, ambient shedding при исчерпании 2.0ms, SoundscapeEvents.DropPendingAmbient().
CRITICAL_PERF_SPIKE: pass >0.5ms пишет MMF telemetry через CrashTelemetryBuffer.ReportCriticalPerformanceSpike(...); в буфере хранится lane hash + stack hash, не строковый stack.
Input curve: normalized = saturate((|v|-0.035)/(1-0.035)), gain = normalized² / max(normalized, eps), итог v * gain. Мелкий шум утапливается, большие mouse deltas не режутся.
Terminal overlay: добавлены AUP sector hash и LOADING NEURAL INTERFACE... через Span<char>/TryFormat, без ToString().

Quadratic input curve:

private static Vector2 ApplyQuadraticLookCurve(Vector2 lookDelta)
{
    float2 raw = new float2(lookDelta.x, lookDelta.y);
    float magnitude = math.length(raw);
    if (magnitude <= LookCurveDeadzone)
        return Vector2.zero;

    float normalized = math.saturate((magnitude - LookCurveDeadzone) / LookCurveRange);
    float quadratic = normalized * normalized;
    float gain = quadratic / math.max(normalized, LookBlendEpsilon);
    return new Vector2(lookDelta.x * gain, lookDelta.y * gain);
}
AAA Cheat


HectonVoxelEngine.cs (line 4245)
: far voxel collider exact bake is skipped through ShouldUseCinematicColliderFake(...); distance is AUP-based, visual mesh remains, expensive physical collider work is avoided.
Freeze-frame dither is shader-side via _HectonFreezeFrameDither for sky/water volume, not CPU simulation.

Time-sliced hydration apply loop:

long loadApplyDeadlineTicks = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() + LoadApplyFrameBudgetTicks;
for (int i = 0; i < _saveableCount; i++)
{
    ISaveable saveable = _saveables[i];
    if (!IsAlive(saveable))
        continue;

    saveable.LoadFromSaveData(data);
    if (i + 1 < _saveableCount && Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() >= loadApplyDeadlineTicks)
    {
        await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(cancellationToken: destroyCancellationToken);
        loadApplyDeadlineTicks = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() + LoadApplyFrameBudgetTicks;
    }
}

Seam Exact Lock

[BurstCompile(FloatPrecision.Standard, FloatMode.Deterministic)]
public struct SnapDualSDFTopCellsToTerrainJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> Sdf;
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> SecondarySdf;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        int z = index / SdfWidth;
        int x = index - z * SdfWidth;
        float terrainHeight = SampleTerrainHeight(
            SdfOriginAup.x + x * (double)VoxelSizeMeters,
            SdfOriginAup.z + z * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);

        float terrainY = (terrainHeight - (float)SdfOriginAup.y) / VoxelSizeMeters;
        int lowerY = (int)math.floor(terrainY);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY, z, terrainHeight);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY + 1, z, terrainHeight);
    }

    private void WriteExactTerrainDensity(int x, int y, int z, float terrainHeight)
    {
        if (y < 0 || y >= SdfHeight) return;
        int i = x + y * SdfWidth + z * SdfWidth * SdfHeight;
        float density = terrainHeight - (float)(SdfOriginAup.y + y * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);
        Sdf[i] = density;
        SecondarySdf[i] = density;
    }
}

3. AUDIO MUFFLING TRANSITION: Moving through an Airlock triggers a 1.5s Audio Mixer Snapshot Lerp. Dry rooms must aggressively LowPass the ocean roar.

5. BULKHEAD SMOOTHSTEP: Emergency doors slide shut using Mathf.SmoothStep over 0.5s. Trigger a heavy PlayAtPoint "Clang" at t=1.0. NO Animators.
6. HULL CONDENSATION SHADER: Base interiors > 2000m deep must increase _Smoothness and blend a droplet normal map to look "sweaty".
7. DIRTY-REGION BFS REBUILD: If a pipe severs, run the Burst BFS ONLY on the affected IslandID. Do not recalculate the whole 100-node base.
8. VISUAL TILT (FAKE PHYSICS): Flooded rooms apply a Quaternion.Euler offset to the root visual mesh. DO NOT touch Rigidbody.centerOfMass.
9. RUPTURE GROAN INTERVALS: Use math.noise(Time.time) to randomly trigger metal stress audio when integrity < 50%. No C# timers.
10. DYNAMIC PIPE RUST: Ruptured pipes must lerp their vertex color to Orange over 300 seconds using a shader time offset.[III. ADVANCED ARCHITECTURE EXPANSION]

142 previous messages
What Was Wrong
PlayerCriticalProceduralAudioRenderer.cs still had hull FM carrier/modulator synthesis, cave reverb could fall back to enclosure sampling, tool cavitation was effectively silent, and sonar tap data could be read from publish buffers instead of a worker-owned snapshot.

What I Did
Edited 
PlayerCriticalProceduralAudioRenderer.cs
:

Added worker-owned sonar tap snapshot to prevent mid-block tap tearing.
Removed hull FM carrier/modulator branch; authored 2D hull loop remains pitch-modulated 0.8..1.2.
Cave reverb now uses fake volume mix only: 0.8 inside VoxelVolumes, 0.2 outside.
Added tool overheat cavitation hash-noise bursts into _bubbleScratch.
O2 tinnitus now uses 8 kHz phase accumulation with exponential player-stress scaling.
Updated smoke gates in:


DSPThreadSafetySmokeTester.cs

AdvancedAcousticsSmokeTester.cs
DSP Math
O2 tinnitus:

phase += 8000 * invSampleRate;
phase -= floor(phase);
sample = sin(2π * phase)
       * saturate(o2Stress^2 * lerp(1, 2.35, 1 - exp(-3.4 * playerStress)))
       * 0.045;
Depth LPF:

mix = saturate((depthMeters - 500) / 4500);
cutoff = lerp(sampleRate * 0.45, 380, mix);

AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: THE INQUISITOR OF VRAM
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Addressables, Zero-GC, Awaitable, ColdTick)[0. SITREP: THE MEMORY LEAK TERROR]
If we don't aggressively unload memory, the MX350 will crash in 10 minutes. You will build the ruthless memory governor.

[I. MANDATORY RECONNAISSANCE]
1. Inspect AssetLifecycleGovernor.cs and VRAMPressureMonitor.cs.

[II. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: MEMORY DICTATORSHIP]
1. ADDRESSABLES REF-COUNT PURGE: Ensure AssetLifecycleGovernor forcibly calls Addressables.Release on any chunk asset that is > 1500m away from the Player AUP in ColdTick (1Hz).
2. AWAITABLE CHUNK STREAMING: Chunk loading must use await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(). Implement a hard budget: if chunk gen logic takes > 2.0ms in a frame, force a yield.
3. VRAM MIP-MAP DROPPING: In VRAMPressureMonitor, if VRAM > 1.6GB, forcibly lower QualitySettings.globalTextureMipmapLimit to 1 (Half Res). Restore to 0 below 1.2GB.
4. COLD-TICK UNLOADING: Move all chunk-distance checks and release logic to ColdTick. Do not check distances every frame.
5. ORIGIN SHIFT GC GUARD: When HectonFloatingOrigin shifts, force a manual Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets() ONLY if System.GC.CollectionCount(0) indicates a safe threshold.
6. PRE-ALLOCATED BUFFER LOCK: Forbid NativeArray.Resize. All WorldStreaming queues must be pre-allocated at MAX_CAPACITY.
7. RENDER TEXTURE POOLING: Ensure RenderTexturePool immediately reclaims any UI/Visor RTs when the PDA closes.
8. STALE REQUEST CANCELLATION: Implement cancellation tokens in streaming to abort Physics.BakeMesh and chunk gen if the player turns around fast.
9. HYDRATION TIME-SLICING: Base loading from saves MUST time-slice. Budget 4.0ms (Stopwatch.Frequency / 250). Yield if exceeded.
10. CACHE COHERENCY FLUSH: Ensure the 5-artery Mega-Bus (SystemDispatcher) drops AmbientEvents completely if the frame time exceeds the budget.

[III. ADVANCED ARCHITECTURE EXPANSION]
1. Apply the cinematic collider fake: distant voxel volumes skip physical collider construction and keep visual mesh only.

[IV. PHASE 4: THE AUTONOMOUS CRUSADE]
